             text LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE SET search_path='' AS $$ SELECT CASE WHEN 
             p_metric='battery_percent' THEN 'percent' WHEN 
             p_metric='battery_runtime_seconds' THEN 'seconds' WHEN 
             p_metric='battery_warning' THEN 'code' WHEN p_metric='channels_healthy' THEN 
             'count' WHEN p_metric='channels_total' THEN 'count' WHEN 
             p_metric='cooling_operational' THEN 'code' WHEN p_metric='disk_healthy' THEN 
             'code' WHEN p_metric='equipment_operational' THEN 'code' WHEN 
             p_metric='humidity_percent' THEN 'percent' WHEN p_metric='ink_percent' THEN 
             'percent' WHEN p_metric='input_power_available' THEN 'code' WHEN 
             p_metric='load_percent' THEN 'percent' WHEN p_metric='maintenance_warning' 
             THEN 'code' WHEN p_metric='output_power_available' THEN 'code' WHEN 
             p_metric='page_count' THEN 'count' WHEN p_metric='paper_available' THEN 'code' 
             WHEN p_metric='paper_jam' THEN 'code' WHEN p_metric='printer_operational' THEN 
             'code' WHEN p_metric='raid_healthy' THEN 'code' WHEN 
             p_metric='recorder_operational' THEN 'code' WHEN 
             p_metric='storage_available_bytes' THEN 'bytes' WHEN 
             p_metric='storage_percent' THEN 'percent' WHEN p_metric='storage_total_bytes' 
             THEN 'bytes' WHEN p_metric='temperature_kelvin' THEN 'kelvin' WHEN 
             p_metric='toner_percent' THEN 'percent' ELSE 
             public.sowrix_ops_v9_prior_unit(p_metric) END $$;
